Contractor/Qualifying Party:  London Bridge Marine Corp/Fred J. Adler, Vice-Pres.  Mobile Ph. 928-716-
6722; Office Ph. 928-855-1555; Email Fred@LondonBridgeMarine.com  
 
Customer/Owner’s Contact:   White Mountain Lake CRID, 2015 Silver Creek Drive, P.O. Box 90807, White 
Mountain Lake, AZ 85912.  Contact:  Pat Stevens, Administrator, WML. CRID.  Office Ph. 928-532-8099; cell 
602-677-3289. 
 
Response to RFP – White Mountain Lake Boat Docks – MRRA Pjts 
MRRA = Maintenance, Repairs, Replacement, and Alteration 
 
History/Rationale/Site Evaluation/Recommendations/Cost Estimates:   
 
History:  London Bridge Marine Corp (formerly Adler Marine) is Arizona’s first Marine Contractor; and, in fact 
helped the AZ ROC write the License Requirements for this new class of Contractor.  We have since upgraded 
to an A-General Engineering License in order to perform upland work away from the water as needed.  In early 
1997, we performed marine construction work for Navajo County at Cholla Lake, supplying and installing dock 
systems there. 
 
As early as year 2000, and then again in 2001 and 2005, Mr. Lem Cooke, developer of Silver Creek Properties 
contracted London Bridge Marine Corp for direction on acquiring and installing boat docks at White Mountain  
Lake.  The Developer acquired dock materials from SuperDeck Boat Docks, assembled, and installed the docks

<!-- page 245 -->
2 
 
utilizing “others” not associated with London Bridge Marine. 
 
In June of 2018, we were contacted by WML CRID “Citizen Volunteers who asked us to look at the existing 
SuperDeck Docks which had suffered damage over the years from dragging them up and down the lake shore as 
water level fluctuates.  Being a SuperDeck authorized installer for over 30-years we provided a courtesy 
inspection on Wednesday July 11, followed by a discussion meeting with the Clerk of the Board at your 
Offices.  This Proposal is being submitted with Ms. Melissa Buckley’s approval. 
 
Rationale:  London Bridge Marine Corp remains a SuperDeck Dealer and Installer.  We are the most qualified, 
licensed contractor to work on this product and obtain materials from the Factory, if not in our current 
inventory.  It was a pleasure to be able to make a courtesy inspection of the WML CRID facility at no cost to 
anyone.  The fact we have a large quantity of serviceable, pre-owned SuperDeck matching dock floats and 
decks recently taken in trade from the City of Tempe during our work on Tempe Town Lake, may very well 
benefit the WML CRID. 
 
Site Evaluation Findings:  We found the dock systems at White Mountain Lake functional; but, in need of repair 
and alteration to keep them in service, in a safer more manageable manner, that eliminates the constant need to 
drag them up and down the slope wherein they get damaged.  (We also found that the original installation, albeit 
sincere, did not fully follow the manufacturer’s instructions, which is something we would like to see 
corrected). 
 
Pertaining to safety and improving the user experience, the fact we have two truckloads of nearly identical 
docks on hand that can be utilized to enhance and reconfigure the dock system is a great opportunity for LBM 
to work with the WML CRID to simplify management of the dock, minimize damage, and make it more useable 
for residents during varying water levels.   
 
Recommendations:  Utilizing the serviceable SuperDeck Dock Systems we promised the City we would 
repurpose elsewhere, we have drawn out two recommended approaches to make the needed repairs, 
improvements and alterations to the existing boat docks. 
 
Option-1:  Creates a “self-tending’ dock spud pile anchoring system that eliminates the need to drag the docks 
in and out of the water.  It enhances, reconfigures, and relocates all the docks into a much better experience for 
both management and the users.  The fact it is self-tending, it will require much less attention and effort from 
the managers and maintenance persons assigned to the facility.  The user experience will be much better and 
safer.  The docks will be able to fulfill their useful lifespan, and keep it in place where it can be maintained for 
years to come. 
 
Option-1 - Base Costs:  Materials fob job site = $114,175.00. Mgt, Insurance, Labor, Prep, and Site Work, Per 
Diem, Mob, and Demob = $85,000.00.  Total = $199,175.00 (plus any applicable AZ TPT, Bonds, Permits). 
 
 
Option-2:  Accomplishes the same as Option-1, but is utilizes a combination Pole and Cable Winch System to 
keep it anchored.  It will require regular monitoring of water level changes, and modest adjustments to the six 
(6) winch stations as the water rises and falls.  Not requiring the extra-long spud poled (36-ft) saves on costs; 
but does incur the cost of special winches, stainless steel cable, winch stands, and new bottom anchors. 
 
Option-2 – Base Costs:  Materials fob job site = $104,591.00.  Mgt, Insurance, Labor, Prep, Site Work, Mob, 
Per Diem, and Demob = $75,000.00.  Total = $179,591.00 (plus any applicable AZ TPT, Bonds, Permits).

<!-- page 246 -->
3 
 
Optional Launch/Loading Dock:  Both Option 1 and 2 Concept Drawings show an “Optional Launch Loading 
Dock” that we can supply and install when performing either Option #1 or Option #2 above.  We have enough 
of the Tempe Boat Docks to do that, which will greatly assist older boaters in launch and retrieving their boats; 
and, may even free up the courtesy boat slips to be turned into revenue generating additional rental slips. 
 
Optional Launch/Loading Dock - Base Costs:  Materials fob job site = $40,825.00. Mgt, Insurance, Labor, Prep, 
and Site Work, Per Diem, Mob, and Demob = $15,000.00.  Total = $55,825.00 (plus any applicable AZ TPT, 
Bonds, Permits). 
 
Timing:  Now is the time to perform the work described above, as the water level is down and the weather is 
supportive; and, we have the necessary matching docks systems available.  If either one of these three factors 
changes, we cannot provide the proposed solutions as outlined.  It is important that the work be performed in 
the late August to early October timeframe; so, “time is of the essence” please. 
 
Sole-Source Potential:  In the past, the City of Tempe has authorized London Bridge Marine to provide 
services to Tempe Town Lake, requiring that the Contractor match the existing SuperDeck dock systems.  The 
fact is although there is local talent, there is no one with the proper SuperDeck Factory Liaison/Installer 
authorizations; nor is there anyone with specialized equipment, materials and parts on hand to effect work per 
factory standards.  This specialized product availability/knowledge with over 30-years’ experience in this 
business (some of which was with Navajo County), makes London Bridge Marine Corp, a legitimate, legal, sole 
source supplier who only can affect the type of maintenance, repair, replacement and alteration (MRRA) 
projects so desperately needed at this residential recreational facility. 
 
Please consider this sole source potential as we have no interest in writing a scope of work for other, 
inexperienced, land based contractors to attempt to perform.  Much of the problems that have been plaguing this 
facility are a result of trying to save money at the expense of safety, management, maintenance and longevity. 
 
Our proposals and basic costs are outline below.  Please review in concert with the two attached Concept 
Drawings; and, if the Board feels our approach has a strong potential to solve problems for the WML CRID 
residents and their guests, please have Ms. Buckley advise me so that we can proceed further in a timely 
manner. 
 
If the Board would like me to be present when this is discussed at the next Board Meeting, please advise me so 
that I can make best efforts to attend.  Thank you. 
 
Respectfully Submitted by, 
 
Frederick J. Adler – V.P. London Bridge Marine Corp.
